Two signed decisions had one agreeing vote and were\\n' +\n    'still marked `DECIDED`. This module is the predicate, its deterministic negative fixtures, and a scanner over\\n' +\n    'the signed receipts.\\n' +\n    '\\n' +\n    'Rules (each one is a refusal with its own code):\\n' +\n    '    VALUE_NOT_BOOLEAN            the declared value is not a real bool (`1` is not `True`)\\n' +\n    '    STATE_NOT_DECIDED            a property that is not DECIDED cannot be counted as decided\\n' +\n    '    REREAD_EVIDENCE_MISSING      `resolved_by` is asserted without `reread_refs`\\n' +\n    '    LANE_ID_DUPLICATE            the same lane voted twice; only one vote per lane counts\\n' +\n    '    STALE_CANDIDATE_VOTE         a vote names a candidate other than the effective one\\n' +\n    '    SIGNED_VOTE_QUORUM_FAIL      fewer than `quorum_min` valid votes agree with the declared value\\n' +\n    '\\n' +\n    'r27/L1 repair (external challenge 7.1 — three false greens). A repair vote — an entry of `votes_r25`, the\\n' +\n    'set that decides a repaired property — is countable ONLY when the whole binding is present, typed and\\n' +\n    're-derived. Each shortfall is its own refusal:\\n' +\n    '    VOTE_TYPE_INVALID            `type(vote) is not bool` (JSON `1`, `\"true\"`, `1.0` are not votes)\\n' +\n    '    MISSING_CANDIDATE_BINDING    a required field of the vote is absent/empty, so the vote binds nothing\\n' +\n    '    PROCESS_IDENTITY_MISSING     `--strict` only: `session_id` / `process_id` absent (see R26-01_GAP)\\n' +\n    \"
